## `AGO-2013-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Melt-Index Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Resin Melt Flow Rate & GPC Molecular Weight Distribution
- **Intro:** Melt-Index Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Resin Melt Flow Rate & GPC Molecular Weight Distribution — Verify Steady-State Process Conditions: Reactor temperature: setpoint ± 0.5°C; reactor pressure: setpoint ± 0.1 bar; feed rate: setpoint ± 0.5% of nominal flow (e.g., 1000 kg/h ±…
- **Key tolerance:** Reactor temperature: setpoint ± 0.5°C; reactor pressure: setpoint ± 0.1 bar; feed rate: setpoint ± 0.5% of nominal flow (e.g., 1000 kg/h ± 5 kg/h).
- **Standards:** `ISO 1133-1:2022`, `ISO 16014-1:2012`, `ASTM D1238-23 Standard Test Method for Melt Flow Rates of Thermo`, `ISO 9001:2015`

## `AGO-2013-MGT-R4-MGT-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `MGT`
- **Role:** Pyrophoric-TEAL Safety Officer
- **Scenario:** Shift Handover, Material Staging Verification & Bottleneck Takt-Time Balancing SOP for Polyethylene/Polypropylene Gas-Phase Fluidized Bed Reactor
- **Intro:** Pyrophoric-TEAL Safety Officer — Shift Handover, Material Staging Verification & Bottleneck Takt-Time Balancing SOP for Polyethylene/Polypropylene Gas-Phase Fluidized Bed Reactor — Initiate Shift Handover and Verify Reactor Mode: Reactor pressure must be within 2.0–2.5 MPa; temperature within 80–110°C; TEAL storage pressure within 0.2–0.5 MPa.

- **Standards:** `ANSI/ISA-101.01-2015 Human-Machine Interfaces`, `ISO 9001:2015`, `ISO 45001:2018`, `IEC 61511:2016`
